d < numGpus; d += localSize) {\n            devs.emplace_back();\n            devs.back().id = d;\n        }\n    }\n    const int nd = (int)devs.size();\n\n    // --- Domain decomposition: contiguous body ranges per GPU worker ---\n    std::vector<int> workersPerRank(nranks);\n    MPI_Allgather(&nd, 1, MPI_INT, workersPerRank.data(), 1, MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    long totalWorkers = 0, myFirstWorker = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n        if (r == rank) myFirstWorker = totalWorkers;\n        totalWorkers += workersPerRank[r];\n    }\n    auto workerStart = [&](long w) { return (int)((w * (long)n) / totalWorkers); };\n\n    std::vector<int> counts3(nranks), displs3(nranks);\n    {\n        long w = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n            const int lo = workerStart(w);\n            w += workersPerRank[r];\n            const int hi = workerStart(w);\n            counts3[r] = 3 * (hi - lo);\n            displs3[r] = 3 * lo;\n        }\n    }\n    const int rankLo = displs3[rank] / 3;\n    const int rankCnt = counts3[rank] / 3;\n    for (int t = 0; t < nd; ++t) {\n        devs[t].i0 = workerStart(myFirstWorker + t);\n        devs[t].cnt = workerStart(myFirstWorker + t + 1) - devs[t].i0;\n    }\n\n    // --- Host buffers (pinned) ---\n    double* hPos = nullptr;   // all positions, packed xyz\n    double* hNew = nullptr;   // this rank's updated positions / velocities\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hPos, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(n, 1)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hNew, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(rankCnt, 1)));\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        hPos[3 * i + 0] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        hPos[3 * i + 1] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        hPos[3 * i + 2] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < rankCnt; ++i) {\n        hNew[3 * i + 0] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.x;\n        hNew[3 * i + 1] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.y;\n        hNew[3 * i + 2] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.z;\n    }\n\n    // --- Device setup (one OpenMP thread per GPU) ---\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nd)\n    {\n        Device& d = devs[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&d.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dPos, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(n, 1)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dPosOut, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(d.cnt, 1)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dVel, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(d.cnt, 1)));\n        if (d.cnt > 0)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.dVel, hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt,\n                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        // Warm-up so context creation is excluded from timing\n        stepKernel<<<1, BLOCK, 0, d.stream>>>(d.dPos, 0, 0, 0, d.dVel, d.dPosOut);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream));\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nd)\n    {\n        Device& d = devs[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        const int grid = (d.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            if (d.cnt > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d.dPos, hPos, sizeof(double) * 3 * n,\n                                           cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, d.stream));\n                stepKernel<<<grid, BLOCK, 0, d.stream>>>(d.dPos, n, d.i0, d.cnt, d.dVel, d.dPosOut);\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), d.dPosOut,\n                                           sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,\n                                           d.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream));\n            }\n#pragma omp barrier\n#pragma omp master\n            MPI_Allgatherv(hNew, counts3[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, hPos, counts3.data(), displs3.data(),\n                           MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n#pragma omp barrier\n        }\n        // Fetch final velocities of owned bodies\n        if (d.cnt > 0)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), d.dVel, sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt,\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    std::vector<double> allVel(3 * (size_t)std::max(n, 1));\n    MPI_Allgatherv(hNew, counts3[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), counts3.data(), displs3.data(),\n                   MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hPos[3 * i + 0], hPos[3 * i + 1], hPos[3 * i + 2]);\n        bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n    }\n\n    for (auto& d : devs) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPos));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPosOut));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dVel));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(d.stream));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hPos));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hNew));\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        int ok = (rank == 0) ? (validateSimulation(bodies) ? 1 : 0) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nranks);\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n            exitCode = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            exitCode = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    fflush(stdout);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_main\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)\n\n# nvcc 12.0 supports host GCC up to 12; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        fflush(stdout);\n    }\n    \n    // Initialize bodies (identically on every rank)\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // --- GPU assignment: split node-local GPUs among node-local ranks ---\n    MPI_Comm localComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm);\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(localComm, &localSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);\n\n    int numGpus = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&numGpus));\n    if (numGpus < 1) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    std::vector<Device> devs;\n    if (localSize >= numGpus) {\n        devs.resize(1);\n        devs[0].id = localRank % numGpus;\n    } else {\n        for (int d = localRank; d < numGpus; d += localSize) {\n            devs.emplace_back();\n            devs.back().id = d;\n        }\n    }\n    const int nd = (int)devs.size();\n\n    // --- Domain decomposition: contiguous body ranges per GPU worker ---\n    std::vector<int> workersPerRank(nranks);\n    MPI_Allgather(&nd, 1, MPI_INT, workersPerRank.data(), 1, MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    long totalWorkers = 0, myFirstWorker = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n        if (r == rank) myFirstWorker = totalWorkers;\n        totalWorkers += workersPerRank[r];\n    }\n    auto workerStart = [&](long w) { return (int)((w * (long)n) / totalWorkers); };\n\n    std::vector<int> counts3(nranks), displs3(nranks);\n    {\n        long w = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n            const int lo = workerStart(w);\n            w += workersPerRank[r];\n            const int hi = workerStart(w);\n            counts3[r] = 3 * (hi - lo);\n            displs3[r] = 3 * lo;\n        }\n    }\n    const int rankLo = displs3[rank] / 3;\n    const int rankCnt = counts3[rank] / 3;\n    for (int t = 0; t < nd; ++t) {\n        devs[t].i0 = workerStart(myFirstWorker + t);\n        devs[t].cnt = workerStart(myFirstWorker + t + 1) - devs[t].i0;\n    }\n\n    // --- Host buffers (pinned) ---\n    double* hPos = nullptr; i < n; ++i) {\n        hPos[3 * i + 0] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        hPos[3 * i + 1] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        hPos[3 * i + 2] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < rankCnt; ++i) {\n        hNew[3 * i + 0] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.x;\n        hNew[3 * i + 1] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.y;\n        hNew[3 * i + 2] = bodies[rankLo + i].vel.z;\n    }\n\n    // --- Device setup (one OpenMP thread per GPU) ---\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nd)\n    {\n        Device& d = devs[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&d.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dPos, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(n, 1)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dPosOut, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(d.cnt, 1)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.dVel, sizeof(double) * 3 * std::max(d.cnt, 1)));\n        if (d.cnt > 0)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.dVel, hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt,\n                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        // Warm-up so context creation is excluded from timing\n        stepKernel<<<1, BLOCK, 0, d.stream>>>(d.dPos, 0, 0, 0, d.dVel, d.dPosOut);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream));\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nd)\n    {\n        Device& d = devs[omp_get_thread_num()];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        const int grid = (d.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            if (d.cnt > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d.dPos, hPos, sizeof(double) * 3 * n,\n                                           cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, d.stream));\n                stepKernel<<<grid, BLOCK, 0, d.stream>>>(d.dPos, n, d.i0, d.cnt, d.dVel, d.dPosOut);\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), d.dPosOut,\n                                           sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost,\n                                           d.stream));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(d.stream));\n            }\n#pragma omp barrier\n#pragma omp master\n            MPI_Allgatherv(hNew, counts3[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, hPos, counts3.data(), displs3.data(),\n                           MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n#pragma omp barrier\n        }\n        // Fetch final velocities of owned bodies\n        if (d.cnt > 0)\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hNew + 3 * (d.i0 - rankLo), d.dVel, sizeof(double) * 3 * d.cnt,\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n    std::vector<double> allVel(3 * (size_t)std::max(n, 1));\n    MPI_Allgatherv(hNew, counts3[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), counts3.data(), displs3.data(),\n                   MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hPos[3 * i + 0], hPos[3 * i + 1], hPos[3 * i + 2]);\n        bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n    }\n\n    for (auto& d : devs) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPos));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPosOut));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dVel));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(d.stream));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hPos));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hNew));\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        int ok = (rank == 0) ? (validateSimulation(bodies) ? 1 : 0) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nranks);\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n            exitCode = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            exitCode = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    fflush(stdout);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_main\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)\n\n# nvcc 12.0 supports host GCC up to 12; (validateSimulation(bodies) ? 1 : 0) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nranks);\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n            exitCode = 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            exitCode = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    fflush(stdout);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_main\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)\n\n# nvcc 12.0 supports host GCC up to 12; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hPos[3 * i + 0], hPos[3 * i + 1], hPos[3 * i + 2]);\n        bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n    }\n\n    for (auto& d : devs) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d.id));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPos));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dPosOut));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d.dVel));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(d.stream));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hPos));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hNew));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (validate) {\n        if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        int ok = (rank == 0) ? (validateSimulation(bodies) ? 1 : 0) : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nranks);\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            exitCode = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    fflush(stdout);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

That includes running with 8 ranks on 4 GPUs and running `./nbody` directly without `mpirun`.\n\n**How the work is split**\n- **MPI:** each rank owns a contiguous block of bodies. Every rank creates the same starting bodies, and after each step they all share updated positions with `MPI_Allgatherv`. Output and the validation result come from rank 0. The energy check is split across ranks and summed with `MPI_Allreduce`.\n- **OpenMP:** the GPUs on a node are divided among the ranks on that node. If a rank gets more than one GPU (for example one rank on a 4-GPU node), it runs one OpenMP thread per GPU. OpenMP also speeds up the CPU-side energy calculation and data packing.\n- **CUDA:** one kernel per step computes the forces and moves the bodies forward. Each GPU thread handles one body and adds up the other bodies' contributions in the same order as the original. The kernel writes new positions to a separate buffer so it never overwrites positions other GPUs are still reading. Velocities stay on the GPU until the end, and host buffers are pinned to speed up copies.\n\n**Why the results match exactly:** I looked at the machine code GCC generates for the original with `-O3 -march=native` and wrote the GPU arithmetic as the same sequence of operations, including where it combines a multiply and add into one rounded step. The build turns off the GPU compiler's own combining (`-fmad=false`) so that order holds.\n\n**Block-size autotuning:** the best GPU block size depends on how many bodies each GPU gets. At startup, before the timer begins, each GPU tries 32, 64, 128 and 256 threads per block on a shortened run and keeps the fastest. It doesn't change the results.\n\n**Performance** (simulation time, 4× RTX 3090):\n\n| Bodies | Steps | Serial CPU | 1 GPU | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 4,096 | 10 | 1236 ms | — | ~30 ms |\n| 65,536 | 5 | — | 2387 ms | ~627 ms |\n| 262,144 | 5 | — | ~33.8 s (half of a 10-step run) | ~9.0 s |\n\nGoing from 1 to 4 GPUs is about 3.8× faster. The kernel is close to the card's double-precision limit. Getting much more would mean a faster but approximate inverse square root, which would break the exact match, so I didn't use one.\n\n**Things to know:**\n- **Final energy:** the result hash matches exactly, but the \"Final energy\" line can differ from the serial code in the last printed digit (123813415.03254**3** vs …03254**5**). This is because the parallel calculation adds the terms in a different order (a relative difference of about 1e-14). Matching it exactly would mean doing that calculation serially, which is far too slow for large inputs.\n- **Build requirements:** CMake now needs version 3.24 or newer. It compiles `nbody.cpp` as CUDA for the GPUs on the build machine. It uses `g++-12` as the host compiler when it's installed, because CUDA 12.0 doesn't support the default GCC 13. It links MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA runtime, with no new external dependencies.\n\nRun it with, for example, `mpirun -np 4 ./build/nbody -n 65536 -s 10`, or with one rank, which then drives all local GPUs."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":664,"cache_read_input_tokens":65109,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":664},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"86836287-f208-451a-afc8-a2c89e4bbb26","uuid":"03fe1574-8584-4389-ad18-28e5f1f19cf4","timestamp":"2026-10-05T13:52:53.167Z","request_id":"req_011CfjCcJzGrQhiQow1wbojG"}
